import React from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';
import LanguageToggle from './LanguageToggle';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { 
  LayoutDashboard, 
  PlusCircle, 
  History, 
  Settings, 
  LogOut, 
  Menu, 
  X,
  CreditCard
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetTrigger } from '@/components/ui/sheet';

interface MainLayoutProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

const MainLayout: React.FC<MainLayoutProps> = ({ children }) => {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [isMobileOpen, setIsMobileOpen] = React.useState(false);

  const navItems = [
    { label: t('dashboard'), path: '/dashboard', icon: LayoutDashboard },
    { label: t('createProject'), path: '/create-project', icon: PlusCircle },
    { label: t('settings'), path: '/settings', icon: Settings },
  ];

  const NavContent = () => (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full py-6">
      <div className="px-6 mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-primary">{t('appName')}</h1>
      </div>
      <nav className="flex-1 px-4 space-y-1">
        {navItems.map((item) => (
          <Button
            key={item.path}
            variant={location.pathname === item.path ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'}
            className="w-full justify-start gap-3 px-4 py-2"
            onClick={() => {
              navigate(item.path);
              setIsMobileOpen(false);
            }}
          >
            <item.icon className="w-5 h-5" />
            <span>{item.label}</span>
          </Button>
        ))}
      </nav>
      <div className="px-4 pt-4 border-t border-border">
        <div className="px-4 py-3 mb-4 rounded bg-muted/50">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider">
            <CreditCard className="w-3 h-3" />
            {t('credits')}
          </div>
          <div className="text-lg font-bold text-primary">{user?.credits} / {user?.plan === 'free' ? 3 : 100}</div>
        </div>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          className="w-full justify-start gap-3 px-4 py-2 text-destructive hover:text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10"
          onClick={() => {
            logout();
            navigate('/');
          }}
        >
          <LogOut className="w-5 h-5" />
          <span>{t('logout')}</span>
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen w-full bg-background">
      {/* Desktop Sidebar */}
      <aside className="hidden md:flex flex-col w-64 shrink-0 border-r border-border bg-card">
        <NavContent />
      </aside>

      {/* Main Content Area */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0 overflow-hidden">
        {/* Mobile Header */}
        <header className="flex md:hidden items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-border bg-card">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Sheet open={isMobileOpen} onOpenChange={setIsMobileOpen}>
              <SheetTrigger asChild>
                <Button variant="ghost" size="icon">
                  <Menu className="w-6 h-6" />
                </Button>
              </SheetTrigger>
              <SheetContent side="left" className="p-0 w-64 bg-card">
                <NavContent />
              </SheetContent>
            </Sheet>
            <span className="font-bold text-lg">{t('appName')}</span>
          </div>
          <LanguageToggle />
        </header>

        {/* Desktop Top Header (optional, usually for language toggle) */}
        <header className="hidden md:flex items-center justify-end px-8 py-3 border-b border-border bg-card h-14">
          <LanguageToggle />
        </header>

        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden p-4 md:p-8">
          <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto w-full">
            {children}
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default MainLayout;

```
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';
import LanguageToggle from '../components/LanguageToggle';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardFooter, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Checkbox } from '@/components/ui/checkbox';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

const Login: React.FC = () => {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const { t, language } = useLanguage();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email || !password) {
      toast.error(language === 'zh' ? '请输入邮箱和密码' : 'Please enter email and password');
      return;
    }
    login(email);
    toast.success(language === 'zh' ? '登录成功' : 'Login successful');
    navigate('/dashboard');
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-background">
      <header className="p-4 flex justify-between items-center">
        <Link to="/" className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-primary flex items-center justify-center text-primary-foreground font-bold rounded-sm">AI</div>
          <span className="font-bold text-xl">{t('appName')}</span>
        </Link>
        <LanguageToggle />
      </header>
      <main className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
        <Card className="w-full max-w-md border border-border shadow-none rounded-sm">
          <CardHeader className="space-y-1">
            <CardTitle className="text-2xl font-bold">{t('login')}</CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>
              {language === 'zh' ? '输入您的账号以访问系统' : 'Enter your credentials to access the system'}
            </CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="email">{language === 'zh' ? '邮箱' : 'Email'}</Label>
                <Input 
                  id="email" 
                  type="email" 
                  placeholder="name@example.com" 
                  value={email} 
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} 
                  className="rounded-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="password">{language === 'zh' ? '密码' : 'Password'}</Label>
                <Input 
                  id="password" 
                  type="password" 
                  value={password} 
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  className="rounded-sm"
                />
              </div>
            </CardContent>
            <CardFooter className="flex flex-col space-y-4">
              <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-11 font-bold">{t('login')}</Button>
              <div className="text-sm text-center text-muted-foreground">
                {language === 'zh' ? '还没有账号？' : "Don't have an account?"}{' '}
                <Link to="/register" className="text-primary font-bold hover:underline">
                  {t('register')}
                </Link>
              </div>
            </CardFooter>
          </form>
        </Card>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Login;

```

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';
import LanguageToggle from '../components/LanguageToggle';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardFooter, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Checkbox } from '@/components/ui/checkbox';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

const Register: React.FC = () => {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [agreed, setAgreed] = useState(false);
  const { register } = useAuth();
  const { t, language } = useLanguage();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email || !password) {
      toast.error(language === 'zh' ? '请输入邮箱和密码' : 'Please enter email and password');
      return;
    }
    if (!agreed) {
      toast.error(language === 'zh' ? '请勾选同意用户协议' : 'Please agree to the Terms of Service');
      return;
    }
    register(email);
    toast.success(language === 'zh' ? '注册成功' : 'Registration successful');
    navigate('/dashboard');
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-background">
      <header className="p-4 flex justify-between items-center">
        <Link to="/" className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-primary flex items-center justify-center text-primary-foreground font-bold rounded-sm">AI</div>
          <span className="font-bold text-xl">{t('appName')}</span>
        </Link>
        <LanguageToggle />
      </header>
      <main className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
        <Card className="w-full max-w-md border border-border shadow-none rounded-sm">
          <CardHeader className="space-y-1">
            <CardTitle className="text-2xl font-bold">{t('register')}</CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>
              {language === 'zh' ? '创建账号开始使用 AI 外贸获客' : 'Create an account to start using AI trade tools'}
            </CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="email">{language === 'zh' ? '邮箱' : 'Email'}</Label>
                <Input 
                  id="email" 
                  type="email" 
                  placeholder="name@example.com" 
                  value={email} 
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  className="rounded-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="password">{language === 'zh' ? '密码' : 'Password'}</Label>
                <Input 
                  id="password" 
                  type="password" 
                  value={password} 
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  className="rounded-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-start space-x-2 pt-2">
                <Checkbox id="terms" checked={agreed} onCheckedChange={(val) => setAgreed(!!val)} />
                <div className="grid gap-1.5 leading-none">
                  <label
                    htmlFor="terms"
                    className="text-sm font-medium leading-none peer-disabled:cursor-not-allowed peer-disabled:opacity-70"
                  >
                    {language === 'zh' ? '我已阅读并同意用户协议与隐私政策' : 'I agree to the User Agreement and Privacy Policy'}
                  </label>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {language === 'zh' ? '请自行修改用户协议与隐私政策以规避法律风险。' : 'Please modify the User Agreement & Privacy Policy yourself to mitigate legal risks.'}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
            <CardFooter className="flex flex-col space-y-4">
              <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-11 font-bold">{t('register')}</Button>
              <div className="text-sm text-center text-muted-foreground">
                {language === 'zh' ? '已有账号？' : 'Already have an account?'}{' '}
                <Link to="/login" className="text-primary font-bold hover:underline">
                  {t('login')}
                </Link>
              </div>
            </CardFooter>
          </form>
        </Card>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Register;

import React from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import MainLayout from '../components/MainLayout';
import { useData, Project } from '../contexts/DataContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';

  Plus, 
  Search, 
  MoreVertical, 
  ExternalLink, 
  Trash2, 
  Clock,
  Briefcase,
  Globe,
  Users
} from 'lucide-react';
import { 
  DropdownMenu, 
  DropdownMenuContent, 
  DropdownMenuItem, 
  DropdownMenuTrigger 
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { format } from 'date-fns';

const Dashboard: React.FC = () => {
  const { projects, deleteProject } = useData();
  const { t, language } = useLanguage();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = React.useState('');

  const filteredProjects = projects.filter(p => 
    p.industry.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) || 
    p.productName.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <MainLayout>
      <div className="space-y-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight text-primary">{t('dashboard')}</h1>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-1">
              {language === 'zh' ? '管理您的外贸获客项目与生成方案' : 'Manage your trade acquisition projects and generated plans'}
            </p>
          </div>
          <Button className="h-11 px-6 font-bold" onClick={() => navigate('/create-project')}>
            <Plus className="w-5 h-5 mr-2" />
            {t('createProject')}
          </Button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 max-w-md">
          <div className="relative flex-1">
            <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" />
            <Input 
              placeholder={language === 'zh' ? '搜索项目或产品...' : 'Search projects or products...'} 
              className="pl-9 rounded-sm"
              value={searchTerm}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {filteredProjects.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {filteredProjects.map((project) => (
              <Card key={project.id} className="group hover:border-primary transition-colors rounded-sm border-border shadow-none h-full flex flex-col">
                <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-start justify-between pb-2">
                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <CardTitle className="text-xl font-bold line-clamp-1">{project.productName}</CardTitle>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      <Clock className="w-3 h-3" />
                      {format(new Date(project.createdAt), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm')}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <DropdownMenu>
                    <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
                      <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-8 w-8">
                        <MoreVertical className="w-4 h-4" />
                      </Button>
                    </DropdownMenuTrigger>
                    <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="rounded-sm">
                      <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => navigate(`/project/${project.id}`)}>
                        <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
                        {language === 'zh' ? '查看详情' : 'View Details'}

                      <DropdownMenuItem 
                        className="text-destructive focus:text-destructive"
                        onClick={() => deleteProject(project.id)}
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
                        {t('delete')}
                      </DropdownMenuItem>
                    </DropdownMenuContent>
                  </DropdownMenu>
                </CardHeader>
                <CardContent className="flex-1 pb-4">
                  <div className="space-y-3 mt-2">

const CreateProject: React.FC = () => {
  const { addProject, saveGeneration } = useData();
  const { user, updateCredits } = useAuth();
  const { t, language } = useLanguage();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    industry: '',
    productName: '',
    market: '',
    customerType: '',
    advantages: '',
  });

  const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
    const { id, value } = e.target;
    setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [id]: value }));
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    
    if (!formData.industry || !formData.productName || !formData.market || !formData.customerType) {
      toast.error(language === 'zh' ? '请填写所有必填字段' : 'Please fill in all required fields');
      return;
    }

    if (user && user.credits <= 0) {

            language === 'zh' ? '合规性与法律纠纷' : 'Compliance and legal disputes'
          ]
        },

          purchaseCycle: language === 'zh' ? '3-6 个月（包含打样、小批量试产与大货招标）' : '3-6 months (Including sampling, trial run, and bulk bidding)',

[My Name]`,

[My Name]`

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-8 max-w-3xl">

import React from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import MainLayout from '../components/MainLayout';
import { useData } from '../contexts/DataContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tabs';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Separator } from '@/components/ui/separator';
import { 
  Copy, 
  Download, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  ArrowLeft,
  FileText,
  Users,
  Mail,
  MessageSquare,
  Lightbulb,
  ChevronRight,
  ExternalLink
} from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

const ProjectResult: React.FC = () => {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const { projects, getProjectGenerations } = useData();
  const { t, language } = useLanguage();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const project = projects.find(p => p.id === id);
  const generation = id ? getProjectGenerations(id) : undefined;

  if (!project || !generation) {
    return (
      <MainLayout>
        <div className="text-center py-20">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">{language === 'zh' ? '未找到项目' : 'Project not found'}</h2>
          <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard')}>{language === 'zh' ? '返回工作台' : 'Back to Dashboard'}</Button>
        </div>
      </MainLayout>
    );
  }

  const handleCopy = (text: string) => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    toast.success(t('copySuccess'));
  };

  const handleExport = () => {

};

生成次数规则\n- 免费版：每月 3 次生成\n- Pro 版：每月 100 次生成\n- 企业版：无限生成\n- 每次生成消耗 1 次额度\n- 次数用尽后弹出升级套餐提示\n\n### 4.3 套餐权限规则\n- 免费版：基础输出\n- Pro 版：高级行业模板 + 导出 PDF\n- 企业版：无限生成 + 多成员 + 专属模板\n\n### 4.4 数据存储规则\n- 用户生成次数用尽 | 弹出升级套餐提示，禁止继续生成 |\n| 用户未填写必填字段 | 提示用户补全必填信息 |\n| AI 生成失败 | 提示用户重试，不消耗生成次数 |\n| 用户忘记密码 | 提供邮箱重置密码功能 |\n| 导出功能失败 | 提示用户重新导出 |\n| 语言切换失败 | 提示用户刷新页面重试 |\n\n## 6. 验收标准\n\n1. 用户能够成功注册和登录\n2.      1 import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
     2 import { translations, Language } from '../translations';
     3 
     4 interface LanguageContextType {
     5   language: Language;
     6   setLanguage: (lang: Language) => void;
     7   t: (key: keyof typeof translations['zh']) => string;
     8 }
     9 
    10 const LanguageContext = createContext<LanguageContextType | undefined>(undefined);
    11 
    12 export const LanguageProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
    13   const [language, setLanguage] = useState<Language>(() => {
    14     const saved = localStorage.getItem('language');
    15     return (saved as Language) || 'zh';
    16   });
    17 
    18   useEffect(() => {
    19     localStorage.setItem('language', language);
    20   }, [language]);
    21 
    22   const t = (key: keyof typeof translations['zh']): string => {
    23     return translations[language][key] || translations['zh'][key];
    24   };
    25 
    26   return (
    27     <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ language, setLanguage, t }}>
    28       {children}
    29     </LanguageContext.Provider>
    30   );
    31 };
    32 
    33 export const useLanguage = () => {
    34   const context = useContext(LanguageContext);
    35   if (context === undefined) {
    36     throw new Error('useLanguage must be used within a LanguageProvider');
    37   }
    38   return context;
    39 };
    40

### ✅ 已可用的功能

- **首页**：展示产品价值、痛点、演示流程、成功案例和价格套餐，支持中英切换
- **登录/注册**：模拟邮箱登录注册流程，已包含用户协议勾选验证
- **工作台（Dashboard）**：可查看、搜索、管理所有创建的项目，支持删除项目
- **创建项目**：填写行业、产品、目标市场等信息，点击生成后有 3 秒 AI 分析动画
- **AI 结果页**：展示完整的 5 大模块（行业分析、客户画像、开发信、跟进话术、成交逻辑），支持一键复制
- **账户设置**：查看套餐状态、剩余额度、修改语言偏好
- **中英双语切换**：任意页面均可一键切换语言，设置会保存到本地

### ⚠️ 当前限制

- **AI 生成是模拟的**：目前生成的内容是基于行业模板的模拟数据，**尚未接入真实的 AI API**（如 GPT/Claude）。如果您需要真实的 AI 生成能力，需要额外接入大模型 API
- **数据存储在浏览器**：所有项目和用户数据保存在 `localStorage` 中，清除浏览器数据会丢失。如需持久化云端存储，需要接入后端数据库（如 Supabase）

### 总结

**作为演示和测试用途，应用是完全可用的**，可以完整体验从注册 → 创建项目 → 生成方案 → 复制内容的全流程。如果您确认这个原型符合需求，下一步可以接入真实的 AI API 来实现智能生成。

  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type',

interface GenerateRequest {
  industry: string;
  productName: string;
  market: string;
  customerType: string;
  advantages?: string;
  language: 'zh' | 'en';
}

serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    return new Response('ok', { headers: corsHeaders });
  }

  try {
    const { industry, productName, market, customerType, advantages, language } = await req.json() as GenerateRequest;

    const apiKey = Deno.env.get('DEEPSEEK_API_KEY');
    if (!apiKey) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'DeepSeek API key not configured' }), {
        status: 500,
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    const systemPrompt = language === 'zh'
      ? `你是一位拥有20年经验的资深外贸B2B销售顾问。你的任务是根据用户提供的产品、行业和市场信息，生成一份专业的外贸获客方案。

你必须严格按照以下JSON格式输出，不要有任何额外文字：
{
  "module1": {
    "focusPoints": ["客户关注点1", "客户关注点2", "客户关注点3", "客户关注点4"],
    "procurementLogic": "采购逻辑分析（2-3句话）",
    "commonRisks": ["风险1", "风险2", "风险3"]
  },
  "module2": {
    "companySize": "目标公司规模描述",
    "decisionMaker": "决策人画像",
    "purchaseCycle": "采购周期描述",
    "conversionPoint": "最容易成交的关键点"
  },
  "module3": {
    "emailV1": "版本1开发信（包含Subject和正文，语气专业自信）",
    "emailV2": "版本2开发信（包含Subject和正文，语气温和建立关系）",
    "emailV3": "版本3开发信（包含Subject和正文，语气直接突出价值）"
  },
  "module4": {
    "day3": "Day 3跟进话术",
    "day7": "Day 7跟进话术",
    "day14": "Day 14跟进话术"
  },
  "module5": {
    "explanation": "为什么这样写的逻辑解释（2-3句话）",
    "tips": ["建议1", "建议2", "建议3"]
  }
}`
      : `You are a senior B2B foreign trade sales consultant with 20 years of experience. Your task is to generate a professional customer acquisition plan based on the user's product, industry, and market information.

You MUST output strictly in the following JSON format with no extra text:
{
  "module1": {
    "focusPoints": ["Focus point 1", "Focus point 2", "Focus point 3", "Focus point 4"],
    "procurementLogic": "Procurement logic analysis (2-3 sentences)",
    "commonRisks": ["Risk 1", "Risk 2", "Risk 3"]
  },
  "module2": {
    "companySize": "Target company size description",
    "decisionMaker": "Decision maker profile",
    "purchaseCycle": "Purchase cycle description",
    "conversionPoint": "Key conversion point"
  },
  "module3": {
    "emailV1": "Version 1 development letter (include Subject and body, professional and confident tone)",
    "emailV2": "Version 2 development letter (include Subject and body, warm relationship-building tone)",
    "emailV3": "Version 3 development letter (include Subject and body, direct value-focused tone)"
  },
  "module4": {
    "day3": "Day 3 follow-up script",
    "day7": "Day 7 follow-up script",
    "day14": "Day 14 follow-up script"
  },
  "module5": {
    "explanation": "Why this approach works (2-3 sentences)",
    "tips": ["Tip 1", "Tip 2", "Tip 3"]
  }
}`;

    const userPrompt = language === 'zh'
      ? `请为以下产品生成外贸获客方案：
- 行业：${industry}
- 产品：${productName}
- 目标市场：${market}
- 客户类型：${customerType}
${advantages ? `- 产品优势：${advantages}` : ''}

请确保开发信英文，其他分析用中文。开发信要真实、专业、有针对性，不要模板化。`
      : `Please generate a foreign trade customer acquisition plan for:
- Industry: ${industry}
- Product: ${productName}
- Target Market: ${market}
- Customer Type: ${customerType}
${advantages ? `- Advantages: ${advantages}` : ''}

All development letters must be in English. Make them authentic, professional, and targeted — not templated.`;

    const response = await fetch('https://api.deepseek.com/chat/completions', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: 'deepseek-chat',
        messages: [
          { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
          { role: 'user', content: userPrompt },
        ],
        temperature: 0.7,
        max_tokens: 4000,
      }),
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      const errorText = await response.text();
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: `DeepSeek API error: ${errorText}` }), {
        status: 500,
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    const data = await response.json();
    const content = data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '';

    // Extract JSON from the response
    let parsedResult;
    try {
      const jsonMatch = content.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
      if (jsonMatch) {
        parsedResult = JSON.parse(jsonMatch[0]);
      } else {
        parsedResult = JSON.parse(content);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Failed to parse AI response', raw: content }), {
        status: 500,
        headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    }

    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true, result: parsedResult }), {
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });

  } catch (error) {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: error.message }), {
      status: 500,
      headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    });
  }
});

NAME: get_pre_code_requirements
RESULTS: <LOGIN_REQUIREMENTS>
principles:
Use Supabase Auth for all login/registration.
Ensure a closed loop: login and logout work; data permissions don’t conflict with features.
Required items: must be complete and verified; higher priority than user requests.
Recommended items: also to be done, but lower priority than user requests.
Protect data security while maximizing access for guests and regular users.

Required:
1. Design a login page.
    - Include registration
    - Add checkboxes for the User Agreement and Privacy Policy.
        - Sample User Agreement & Privacy Policy (Brief one sentence)
        - Inform the user in the finish summary. <user-reminder>Please modify the User Agreement & Privacy Policy yourself to mitigate legal risks.<user-reminder/>
2. Auto-sync new users to profiles on INSERT.
   - Set search_path = public to resolve types correctly.
   - The following template has been validated as a best practice and is compatible with any business requirements. Only the custom registration fields to be synchronized in handle_new_user may be modified. No other changes are allowed, and no additional trigger logic may be added to the auth table.
Template:
```sql
CREATE TYPE public.user_role AS ENUM ('user', 'admin');

CREATE FUNCTION handle_new_user()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public
AS $$
BEGIN
-- Insert a profile synced with fields collected at signup.
INSERT INTO public.profiles (id, email, phone, role)
VALUES (
  NEW.id,
  NEW.email,
  NEW.phone,
  'user'::public.user_role
);
RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

-- Do not add any additional triggers to the auth table.
CREATE TRIGGER on_auth_user_created
  AFTER INSERT ON auth.users
  FOR EACH ROW
  EXECUTE FUNCTION handle_new_user();
```
3. AuthContext / RouteGuard:
    - Always add AuthProvider in @/App.tsx so all pages can access login state (e.g. navbar login/logout).
    - RouteGuard is optional: only add it when authentication is required for most routes.
      - If the app pattern is "only admin needs login, other routes are public", do NOT add RouteGuard. Its default-block-all interception (whitelist-only) will break the app for regular users.
      - When RouteGuard IS needed: review @/contexts/AuthContext.tsx and @/components/common/RouteGuard.tsx, do not create new files. Check PUBLIC_ROUTES and add RouteGuard in @/App.tsx.
4. Show login status and login/logout in navbar(add header in app.tsx); if without a navbar, add a login/logout button, login status on the home page.
5. After a successful login, the user must be redirected to a non-login page.
6. If roles/admin needed:
   - AI must create one example admin account with a randomly generated, strong password (16+ chars, mixed case + digits + symbols). Never use simple passwords unless the user explicitly specifies one. Output the credentials in the finish summary.
   - Account creation flow (two-step, fully automated via script):
     1. Register the account using the normal signUp API (triggers handle_new_user to insert the profiles row with role='user').
     2. Immediately after, use the service_role key (Supabase admin client) to update the profiles table and set role='admin'. Do NOT ask the user to change the role manually.
     Example script pattern:
     ```javascript
     const supabaseAdmin = createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SERVICE_ROLE_KEY); // service_role key, server-side only
     const { data: authData } = await supabase.auth.signUp({ email: `${username}@miaoda.com`, password });
     await supabaseAdmin.from('profiles').update({ role: 'admin' }).eq('id', authData.user.id);
     ```
   - The script must be executed automatically as part of the setup; log the resulting credentials (username + password) to the console.
   - Admin sees Admin entry in navbar.
   - Admin page: manage user roles (edit others).
   - profiles permissions:
     1. Create helper `has_role` to prevent infinite recursion detected in policy for relation "profiles"
     2. Admins have full access
     3. Users can view own data
     4. Users can edit own data except `role`
     5. Use `public_profiles` view for shareable info
     Here is an example:
```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION has_role(uid uuid, role_name text)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM profiles p
    WHERE p.id = uid AND p.role = role_name::user_role
  );
$$;

-- Profiles policies
CREATE POLICY "Admins have full access to profiles" ON profiles
  FOR ALL TO authenticated USING (has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin'));

CREATE POLICY "Users can view their own profile" ON profiles
  FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (auth.uid() = id);

CREATE POLICY "Users can update their own profile" ON profiles
  FOR UPDATE TO authenticated USING (auth.uid() = id)
  WITH CHECK (role IS NOT DISTINCT FROM (SELECT role FROM profiles WHERE id = auth.uid()));

CREATE VIEW public_profiles AS
  SELECT id, role FROM profiles;
```

7. Anonymous login (if needed):
   - On first visit, generate a UUID v4 as the anonymous credential ID and persist it in localStorage under the key `anon_credential_id`. This is NOT the Supabase auth.uid — it is an app-level identifier.
   - Create a table `anon_credential_map (credential_id uuid PRIMARY KEY, auth_uid uuid REFERENCES profiles(id))` to store the mapping. RLS: only the Edge Function's service-role key may read/write this table.
   - Create a Supabase Edge Function `anon-auth` that:
     1. Accepts `{ credential_id: string }` in the request body.
     2. Queries `anon_credential_map` for the given credential_id.
     3. If found: calls `supabaseAdmin.auth.admin.generateLink({ type: 'magiclink', email })` to generate a token for the existing user.
     4. If not found: first calls `supabaseAdmin.auth.admin.createUser({ email: 'anon_<credential_id>@miaoda.com', password: <random strong password>, email_confirm: true })` to register the user, inserts the credential_id → auth.uid row into `anon_credential_map`, then calls `generateLink` to generate a token.
     5. Returns `{ hashed_token }` (from `data.properties.hashed_token`) to the client.
   - On the client, call `supabase.auth.verifyOtp({ token_hash: hashed_token, type: 'magiclink' })` to activate the session.
   - The Edge Function must use the service-role key (server-side only, never exposed to the client).
   - The credential_id must never be sent to any third party and must only be used for this Edge Function call.
   - Note: the `on_auth_user_created` trigger will fire for anonymous users too; ensure the profiles table allows null email and phone.


Recommended:
1. If no method specified, use username + password.
2. Login must check `supabase_verification`; show phone/email verification UI if enabled.
3. If no verification is needed, auto-login after signup and go back to the last page.


Tech limits:
1. Unregistered users can’t set roles; register first via signUp, then use the service_role key to update role programmatically.
2. Single account system; multi-device handled by roles.
3. Password changes/add accounts via Edge Functions (do not use trigger for auth→profiles).
4. Do not insert into profiles directly; only trigger sync adds rows.
5. Only profiles.id may reference auth.users(id); other tables must reference profiles(id).
6. use the `supabase_verification` tool to set up verification
7. Only username in email field; no phone.
8. Do not allow login with both email and username. Use username. Store email in the profiles table.
9. AI-registered or AI-created accounts must use randomly generated, strong passwords (16+ chars, mixed uppercase + lowercase + digits + symbols). Never use simple or guessable passwords unless the user explicitly specifies the password.

Finish Summary Requirements (MANDATORY):
In the final reply to the user, you MUST include a summary section covering all applicable items:
1. List all login methods implemented (e.g., username-password, Google SSO, phone OTP, email).

New String: ```
    setLoading(true);
    
    try {
      const newProject = addProject(formData);
      
      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('deepseek-generate', {
        body: {
          industry: formData.industry,
          productName: formData.productName,
          market: formData.market,
          customerType: formData.customerType,
          advantages: formData.advantages,
          language,
        },
      });

      if (error) {
        const errorMsg = await error?.context?.text?.();
        console.error('Edge function error:', errorMsg || error.message);
        throw new Error(errorMsg || error.message);
      }

      if (!data?.success || !data?.result) {
        throw new Error('Invalid response from AI service');
      }

      const result = data.result;

      const generation = {
        id: Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9),
        projectId: newProject.id,
        module1: {
          focusPoints: Array.isArray(result.module1?.focusPoints) ? result.module1.focusPoints : [],
          procurementLogic: result.module1?.procurementLogic || '',
          commonRisks: Array.isArray(result.module1?.commonRisks) ? result.module1.commonRisks : [],
        },
        module2: {
          companySize: result.module2?.companySize || '',
          decisionMaker: result.module2?.decisionMaker || '',
          purchaseCycle: result.module2?.purchaseCycle || '',
          conversionPoint: result.module2?.conversionPoint || '',
        },
        module3: {
          emailV1: result.module3?.emailV1 || '',
          emailV2: result.module3?.emailV2 || '',
          emailV3: result.module3?.emailV3 || '',
        },
        module4: {
          day3: result.module4?.day3 || '',
          day7: result.module4?.day7 || '',
          day14: result.module4?.day14 || '',
        },
        module5: {
          explanation: result.module5?.explanation || '',
          tips: Array.isArray(result.module5?.tips) ? result.module5.tips : [],
        },
      };

      saveGeneration(generation);
      if (user) updateCredits(user.credits - 1);
      
      toast.success(language === 'zh' ? '方案生成成功' : 'Plan generated successfully');
      navigate(`/project/${newProject.id}`);
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('Generation error:', error);
      toast.error(language === 'zh' ? `生成失败: ${error.message || '请稍后重试'}` : `Generation failed: ${error.message || 'Please try again later'}`);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }

   135       });
   136     }
   137 
   138     const data = await response.json();

   140 
   141     // Extract JSON from the response
   142     let parsedResult;
   143     try {
   144       const jsonMatch = content.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
   145       if (jsonMatch) {
   146         parsedResult = JSON.parse(jsonMatch[0]);
   147       } else {
   148         parsedResult = JSON.parse(content);
   149       }
   150     } catch (e) {
   151       return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Failed to parse AI response', raw: content }), {
   152         status: 500,
   153         headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   154       });
   155     }
   156 
   157     return new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true, result: parsedResult }), {
   158       headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   159     });
   160 
   161   } catch (error) {
   162     return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: error.message }), {
   163       status: 500,
   164       headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   165     });
   166   }

      const result = data.result;

      // Helper to safely convert any value to string
      const safeString = (val: any): string => {
        if (typeof val === 'string') return val;
        if (val && typeof val === 'object') {
          // Handle object like {Subject: "...", 正文: "..."}
          return Object.entries(val).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join('\n\n');
        }
        return String(val || '');
      };

      const generation = {
        id: Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9),
        projectId: newProject.id,
        module1: {
          focusPoints: Array.isArray(result.module1?.focusPoints) ? result.module1.focusPoints : [],

    79         if (typeof val === 'string') return val;
    80         if (val && typeof val === 'object') {

     1 import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
     2 import { useAuth } from './AuthContext';
     3 
     4 export interface Project {
     5   id: string;
     6   userId: string;
     7   industry: string;
     8   productName: string;
     9   market: string;
    10   customerType: string;
    11   advantages?: string;
    12   createdAt: string;
    13 }
    14 
    15 export interface Generation {
    16   id: string;
    17   projectId: string;
    18   module1: { focusPoints: string[]; procurementLogic: string; commonRisks: string[] };
    19   module2: { companySize: string; decisionMaker: string; purchaseCycle: string; conversionPoint: string };
    20   module3: { emailV1: string; emailV2: string; emailV3: string };
    21   module4: { day3: string; day7: string; day14: string };
    22   module5: { explanation: string; tips: string[] };
    23 }
    24 
    25 interface DataContextType {
    26   projects: Project[];
    27   generations: Generation[];
    28   addProject: (project: Omit<Project, 'id' | 'userId' | 'createdAt'>) => Project;
    29   getProjectGenerations: (projectId: string) => Generation | undefined;
    30   saveGeneration: (generation: Generation) => void;
    31   deleteProject: (projectId: string) => void;
    32 }
    33 
    34 const DataContext = createContext<DataContextType | undefined>(undefined);
    35 
    36 export const DataProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
    37   const { user } = useAuth();
    38   const [projects, setProjects] = useState<Project[]>(() => {
    39     const saved = localStorage.getItem('projects');
    40     return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : [];
    41   });
    42   const [generations, setGenerations] = useState<Generation[]>(() => {
    43     const saved = localStorage.getItem('generations');
    44     return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : [];
    45   });
    46 
    47   useEffect(() => {
    48     localStorage.setItem('projects', JSON.stringify(projects));
    49   }, [projects]);
    50 
    51   useEffect(() => {
    52     localStorage.setItem('generations', JSON.stringify(generations));
    53   }, [generations]);
    54 
    55   const addProject = (projectData: Omit<Project, 'id' | 'userId' | 'createdAt'>) => {
    56     const newProject: Project = {
    57       ...projectData,
    58       id: Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9),
    59       userId: user?.id || 'guest',
    60       createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    61     };
    62     setProjects([newProject, ...projects]);
    63     return newProject;
    64   };
    65 
    66   const getProjectGenerations = (projectId: string) => {
    67     return generations.find((g) => g.projectId === projectId);
    68   };
    69 
    70   const saveGeneration = (generation: Generation) => {
    71     setGenerations([generation, ...generations.filter(g => g.projectId !== generation.projectId)]);
    72   };
    73 
    74   const deleteProject = (projectId: string) => {
    75     setProjects(projects.filter(p => p.id !== projectId));
    76     setGenerations(generations.filter(g => g.projectId !== projectId));
    77   };
    78 
    79   return (
    80     <DataContext.Provider value={{ projects, generations, addProject, getProjectGenerations, saveGeneration, deleteProject }}>
    81       {children}
    82     </DataContext.Provider>
    83   );
    84 };
    85 
    86 export const useData = () => {
    87   const context = useContext(DataContext);
    88   if (context === undefined) {
    89     throw new Error('useData must be used within a DataProvider');
    90   }
    91   return context;
    92 };

  const safeRender = (val: any): string => {
    if (typeof val === 'string') return val;
    if (val && typeof val === 'object') {
      return Object.entries(val).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join('\n\n');
    }
    return String(val || '');
  };

  // Helper to safely render array
  const safeArray = (arr: any): string[] => {
    if (!Array.isArray(arr)) return [];
    return arr.map(item => safeRender(item));
  };

  if (!project || !generation) {
    return (
      <MainLayout>
        <div className="text-center py-20">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">{language === 'zh' ? '未找到项目' : 'Project not found'}</h2>
          <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard')}>{language === 'zh' ? '返回工作台' : 'Back to Dashboard'}</Button>
        </div>
      </MainLayout>
    );
  }
```

</action>
